# Hand-written BOCF readings for ordinals past the converter's reach.
#
# One point per line:
#
#   <matrix> = <BOCF LaTeX> [= <another BOCF form>] @ <value> <R|->
#
# The matrix may be written in any form `from_display` accepts, and the first
# reading is the one the BOCF panel shows; further columns are kept for
# reference only. Order does not matter, and a repeated matrix keeps its first
# reading.
#
# The value and the flag are generated, not written: `<value>` is where the
# walk reaches the point on its curve, and `R` says it stops on it exactly
# (`-` that it steps over it). They are what the panel's lookup compares, so
# they must be regenerated whenever the table changes — see `regenerate_values`
# in `bocf_table.rs`.
#
# Generated from a hand-written table kept outside the repository; regenerate
# this file instead of editing it directly.
